Treasury Yields Fall — The yield on the US 10-year Treasury note fell to 4.67% on Monday, supported by lower oil prices and easing inflation concerns as hopes grew that tensions in the Middle East could ease. US President Trump said negotiations with Iran would begin on Monday afternoon and canceled a planned attack on the country. Investors are also awaiting a fresh batch of US economic data this week, including the closely watched employment report, for further clues on the strength of the labor market. Last week, the Fed left interest rates unchanged, although three policymakers dissented. Markets are now pricing in a roughly 63% probability of a 25bps rate hike in September, down from around 80% before the policy decision, as Chair Kevin Warsh provided little forward guidance on the outlook for interest rates. Separately, reports indicated that Warsh is considering reducing the number of Federal Reserve policy meetings from the current eight per year.

Mexico Manufacturing Confidence Remains Unchanged in July — Mexico's manufacturing business confidence held steady at 48.0 in July 2026, remaining below the 50-point threshold for the 17th consecutive month. Firms' assessment of the country's current economic situation edged up 0.2 points to 48.3, while expectations for the economy improved 0.3 points to 52.1. ### 2026-08-03 20:10:56  #其他

【中國信通院：2025年我國人工智慧產業規模超1.2萬億元】中國資訊通訊研究院持續開展人工智慧產業研究，並開展2025年人工智慧產業規模測算。經測算，2025年我國人工智慧產業規模超1.2萬億元，同比增長40%。截至2026年6月，人工智慧企業數量超6600家，全球佔比達到15%，已形成覆蓋基礎底座、模型框架、行業應用的完整產業體系。人工智慧產業鏈分為基礎層、模型框架層和應用層。2025年，我國人工智慧基礎層產業規模同比增長59%，佔比為38%；人工智慧模型框架層規模同比增長189%，佔比為7%；人工智慧應用層規模同比增長22%，佔比為55%。下一步，中國信通院將持續夯實產業監測基礎，明晰產業發展格局，實時掌握產業發展動態，精準識別風險與機遇，為政府科學決策和行業健康發展提供有力支撐。（中國信通院）

【CAICT: China's AI industry to exceed 1.2 tln yuan in 2025】China Academy of Information and Communications Technology (CAICT) estimates China's AI industry will exceed 1.2 tln yuan in 2025, up 40% YoY. As of June 2026 there are over 6,600 AI firms in China, about 15% of the global total. CAICT divides the industry into base, model/framework and application layers: in 2025 the base layer grew 59% YoY and accounted for 38% of the industry, the model/framework layer grew 189% YoY and accounted for 7%, and the application layer grew 22% YoY and accounted for 55%.

【美聯儲決議疊加日元反彈 美元進一步下跌】受美日聯合干預匯市支撐日元，以及上週美聯儲議息會議引發美元拋售影響，美元週一延續跌勢。彭博美元即期指數盤中一度跌至一個月低點，對G-10貨幣下跌0.5%。不過，截至倫敦時間上午11時20分，該指數已收復大部分跌幅，跌幅收窄至0.1%。與此同時，美日聯手干預匯市後，日元自四十年來低點反彈。美國財政部長斯科特·貝森特表示如有必要，美國會毫不猶豫地再次介入市場，這一點確認日本在遏制日元過度貶值方面擁有一個實力強大的合作伙伴。美元兌日元下跌0.3%。不過，美元此輪跌勢的根源可追溯至上週。當時，美聯儲決定維持利率不變，市場因此對新任主席凱文·沃什遏制通脹的決心產生疑問。美元指數上週累計下跌1.3%。ING外匯策略師Francesco Pesole表示，“這一切都始於那次美聯儲會議。當時市場普遍做多美元。如果觀察倉位指標就會發現，短線投資者當時幾乎全面大舉押注美元上漲。”

【Analyst: US-Japan intervention prolongs dollar slide but fundamentals may remain USD-positive】USD extended losses on Monday after coordinated US-Japan FX intervention that supported the yen. Traders trace the dollar’s weakness to last week’s Fed decision to hold rates, which has raised questions about new chair Walsh’s ability to fight inflation. ING FX strategist Francesco Pesole said the move began after the Fed meeting and that short-term investors had built large USD long positions. Some strategists say the US Treasury may fund yen purchases with euros rather than dollars to limit further dollar weakness. Pesole added many traders are weighing longer-term USD shorts but called Japanese intervention temporary and said Fed policy will ultimately determine the dollar’s path. Jefferies strategist Mohit Kumar said if oil does not fall sharply, a Fed failure to act on inflation would damage Walsh’s credibility, and that aside from intervention fundamentals remain unfavorable for the yen and supportive of the dollar, with rising pressure for Fed hikes.

### 2026-08-03 19:49:24  #Trading Economics #Markets #Switzerland #Currency #Importance 0

Swiss Franc Stays Near One-Year Low — The Swiss franc traded around $0.81 after weakening to a nearly one-year low of $0.821 on July 28th, as uncertainty surrounding the Middle East conflict remained elevated, while inflation concerns eased. Swiss inflation eased to 0.4% in July from 0.5%, its lowest level in four months, underscoring the limited pass-through from higher energy prices linked to geopolitical tensions. The reading contrasted with the Swiss National Bank's expectation of a modest near-term pickup in inflation, following its decision to hold its policy rate at 0%. The SNB is expected to leave borrowing costs unchanged through year-end, with further cuts remaining a contingency rather than the base case, citing no severe damage to Swiss banks. Meanwhile, investor sentiment improved sharply in July, returning to positive territory for the first time since the conflict began. While most economists see the first SNB rate hike in early 2028, currency markets continue to price in an increase as early as March 2027.

【Fourth national proprietary Chinese medicine procurement finalised; OTC and exclusive varieties fully included】Led by the Hubei provincial medical insurance bureau, the fourth national centralised procurement for proprietary Chinese medicines published full results. The tender set 28 procurement groups and covered 89 commonly used proprietary Chinese medicines, including OTC and exclusive varieties, representing a market of more than CNY45 bln; over 40,000 public medical institutions reported purchase volumes. A total of 344 firms submitted 468 products; 243 firms with 310 product specifications won. Overall average price cuts exceeded 50%. The procurement used a composite scoring model (price 60%, technical evaluation 40%) and introduced reinstatement/supplementary lists and A/B grouping competition, moving away from lowest-price-only selection to incorporate drug quality, clinical value and supply stability. Industry analysts said the round accelerates alignment of in-hospital and retail pricing, will concentrate share with firms that have scale, supply-chain and quality-control advantages, and is likely to accelerate exit by smaller players lacking core barriers.

### 2026-08-03 19:38:18  #Trading Economics #Economy #Switzerland #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 0

Swiss 10-Year Bond Yield Holds Near 0.4% — Switzerland's 10-year government bond yield hovered near 0.4% since July 28th as uncertainty surrounding the Middle East conflict remained elevated, while inflation concerns eased. Swiss inflation eased to 0.4% in July from 0.5%, its lowest level in four months, highlighting the limited pass-through from higher energy prices linked to geopolitical tensions. The reading contrasted with the Swiss National Bank's expectation of a modest near-term pickup in inflation, following its decision to hold its policy rate at 0%. The SNB is expected to leave borrowing costs unchanged throughout the year, with further cuts remaining a contingency rather than the base case, citing no severe damage to Swiss banks. Meanwhile, investor sentiment improved sharply in July, returning to positive territory for the first time since the conflict began. While most economists see the first SNB rate hike in early 2028, currency markets continue to price in an increase as early as March 2027.

US Futures Rise on Monday — Futures tracking US equities were higher on Monday with support from respite for Treasury securities. The S&P 500 rose 0.6%, the Nasdaq 100 was slightly higher, and the Dow added over 400 points. The US refrained from striking Iran over the weekend and oil prices pulled back. The moves drove yields to decrease across the curve as markets readjusted inflationary risks, supporting credit-sensitive equity sectors. Bank holding companies were higher, with JPMorgan, Bank of America, and Goldman Sachs gaining 1% premarket. Meanwhile, Bristol-Myers Squibb surged nearly 10% amid reports that it could be acquired by AstraZeneca. In turn, chip producers extended their volatile momentum as markets consistently reconsider their speculative exposure and overleveraged investors liquidate positions at likely discounts. 【瑞幸咖啡公佈2026年第二季度財報：總淨收入同比增長28.5%，約159億元】8月3日，瑞幸咖啡公佈2026年第二季度財報。資料顯示，二季度公司總淨收入158.86億元人民幣，同比增長28.5%；GAAP營業利潤同比轉為正增長，較去年同期增長22.0%，達21.23億元人民幣，營業利潤率為13.4%，盈利修復態勢進一步鞏固。月均交易客戶數同比增長22.9%，達1.13億，創歷史新高。截至二季度末，全球門店總數達36,310家，累計交易客戶數已近5億。二季度，瑞幸自營門店收入115.64億元人民幣，同比增長26.6%；自營門店門店層面營業利潤為24.69億元人民幣，同比增長25.9%，門店層面營業利潤率為21.3%。聯營門店收入為36.68億元人民幣，同比增長27.9%。GMV同比增長29.8%，達184.00億元人民幣。二季度，瑞幸門店網路規模和增速均領跑行業。二季度淨增門店2714家，其中，中國（含香港）淨增2668家，海外淨增46家。截至二季度末，瑞幸全球門店總數達36310家；其中，中國市場（含香港）門店總數達36087家，包括自營23625家，聯營12462家。在國際市場，瑞幸繼續穩步推進海外業務拓展。截至二季度末，海外門店總數達223家，包括新加坡自營門店89家、美國自營門店20家，馬來西亞加盟門店114家。（新浪科技）

### 2026-08-03 19:20:16  #國際

【日媒披露：日本與烏克蘭聯合開發無人機】據日本《讀賣新聞》8月3日報道，日本與烏克蘭的民間企業正朝著合作開發無人機的方向邁進。日本方面希望藉助烏克蘭在戰火中淬鍊出的“無人機強國”經驗來推動產品化程序，而烏克蘭則盤算著以此作為進軍亞洲市場的切入點。儘管外界對兩國開展防務無人機開發的政府間合作抱有期待，但受制於兩國的法規限制，目前面臨不少阻礙，因此相關合作正以民間為主導先行推進。報道稱，7月6日，各方宣佈成立旨在推動兩國企業在無人機開發領域進行合作的機制——“日烏無人機產業叢集”（JUDC）。該機制旨在將兩國的無人機相關企業緊密聯結起來，促進研發協同。此舉的核心目的，是為災害救援及物流等場景中應用的工業級無人機生產提供助力。目前雖然僅有5家企業加入，但各方對未來的規模擴大抱有很高期待。（參考訊息）

### 2026-08-03 18:58:28  #公司

【駭客攻破比特幣“最安全保險箱” 持續洗劫使用者資產】駭客發現一款比特幣“冷錢包”軟體存在漏洞。這類錢包一直被認為是存放加密貨幣最安全的方式之一，但攻擊者正利用這一漏洞持續盜走價值數千萬美元的加密貨幣。加拿大公司Coinkite Inc.上週末通知Coldcard硬體錢包使用者，保護其比特幣的金鑰生成機制存在安全漏洞，部分錢包已受到影響。Galaxy Research的資料顯示，截至週一，攻擊者已從約5000個錢包中轉走超過1755枚比特幣，按當時市值計算約合1.1億美元。Coldcard是一款硬體錢包，使用者可藉助該裝置將比特幣存放在所謂的“冷錢包”中。由於冷錢包與網際網路隔離，長期以來一直被認為是儲存加密貨幣最安全的方式。

### 2026-08-03 18:50:50  #公司

【5年期大額存單“返場” 利率可達1.75%】近年來，在銀行業淨息差承壓的背景下，國有大行、股份行紛紛壓降成本相對高昂的大額存單。長期限存款產品，尤其是5年期大額存單，曾一度消失在銀行“貨架”上。不過，近期，中國銀行、建設銀行、平安銀行等部分國有大行、股份制銀行重新“上架”5年期大額存單。工商銀行一位客戶經理表示，目前該行5年期大額存單以及存款均有上架，其中大額存款的利率為1.6%，但“額度要靠搶”。農業銀行深圳一支行客戶經理表示，目前該行未有5年期大額存單，但普通存款有5年期產品上架銷售，年利率是1.6%。博通諮詢首席分析師王蓬博表示，部分國有行、股份行恢復發售3年期、5年期大額存單，首要目的還是應對存款短期化趨勢。“當下居民傾向持有活期與短期存款，銀行負債端久期持續縮短，但信貸端仍存在中長期融資需求，資產負債久期錯配風險不斷積累。”王蓬博介紹，機構藉助長期大額存單吸納穩定性更強的核心存款，最佳化負債久期結構。“這類產品重新上架也應該看作銀行階段性負債管理手段，並非負債經營策略轉向。”（每經網）

【Five-year large-denomination CDs return; rates up to 1.75%】Several state-owned and joint-stock banks, including Bank of China, China Construction Bank and Ping An Bank, have relaunched five-year large-denomination certificates of deposit as lenders seek to lengthen liabilities amid NIM pressure and shorter deposit tenors. ICBC client managers say five-year large CDs and standard five-year deposits are on sale at about 1.6% but with limited quotas; Agricultural Bank’s Shenzhen branch offers ordinary five-year deposits at 1.6% while not listing large-denomination CDs. Market checks indicate some issuances reach 1.75%. Botong Consulting chief analyst Wang Pengbo says banks are using long-term large CDs to attract more stable core funding and alleviate asset-liability duration mismatch, framing the reintroductions as temporary liability-management measures rather than a permanent funding strategy shift.

### 2026-08-03 18:45:40  #公司

【微信公眾平臺規範網際網路渠道售卡類服務內容】8月3日，微信公眾平臺運營中心釋出關於規範網際網路渠道售卡類服務內容的公告。中國電信、中國移動、中國聯通於2026年7月31日釋出《關於規範網際網路渠道售卡管理的公告》：為保障使用者權益和資料安全，自2026年8月1日起，使用者在網際網路渠道訂購號卡，只能透過電信運營商官方 App、官方網站等官方渠道辦理，第三方網際網路渠道不再提供電訊號卡辦理相關服務。為規範電話卡銷售業務、防範使用者資訊洩露風險、維護內容生態健康，平臺現就號卡辦理類服務內容作如下規範：一、經營主體准入號卡辦理相關業務僅由電信運營商開展。自本公告發布之日起，平臺不再支援非電信運營商主體從事號卡辦理、售卡引流、代辦開卡等相關經營行為。平臺當前已取消小程式類目“電話卡銷售（中國聯通/中國電信/中國移動）”，同時不再支援從事相關業務的公眾號/服務號認證。二、現存賬號自查整改目前存在相關內容的賬號，請自8月11日前完成自查整改，包括但不限於：修改涉及號卡辦理、售卡引流導向的暱稱、頭像或簡介；刪除相關歷史文章及內容；下線相關自定義選單；移除透過跳轉連結、二維碼、外部頁面等方式引流至第三方售卡渠道的相關內容；關閉或修改相關關鍵詞自動回覆、被關注自動回覆等功能；下線相關小程式號卡辦理、選號、下單、代辦開卡等相關業務功能流程、服務入口及相關頁面。對於限期記憶體量未整改相關服務並繼續經營的公眾號或小程式，將按平臺規則進行處理。

Oil Falls as US-Iran Talks to Resume — Crude oil fell about 5% to below $80 a barrel on Monday after US President Donald Trump called off a planned military strike on Iran and said fresh negotiations with Tehran would begin. Trump said regional allies, including Saudi Arabia, had urged Washington to pursue diplomacy, prompting him to suspend the attack while efforts continue to secure a deal to reopen the Strait of Hormuz. Iran denied holding direct talks with the US but said discussions with Oman were making progress toward improving shipping through the strategic waterway. Elsewhere, Turkey and Iraq extended a key oil pipeline agreement by one year, supporting alternative export routes. Kazakhstan also resumed crude intake through the Caspian Pipeline Consortium after a brief suspension, although exports remain vulnerable to drone attacks targeting tankers. Meanwhile, OPEC+ approved another modest production increase, completing the planned restoration of supply cuts introduced in 2023.

【Williams says inflation likely to ease; Fed will raise rates if it doesn't】Federal Reserve Governor Williams said he remains optimistic inflationary pressures will ease but warned the Fed will not hesitate to raise rates if that does not occur. In a Reuters interview he said if energy prices and trade tariffs have peaked and the economy stays resilient, the main upward drivers of inflation from the past 18 months should wane and disinflationary forces should re-emerge. He is closely watching coming months' core inflation for consistency with a downward trend toward 2% and said the Fed aims to secure a stable 2% rate by 2028. Williams personally expects inflation to fall in H2 this year and to decline further next year, and he reiterated that the current policy stance is positioned to bring inflation back to target but that further tightening would be appropriate if the 2% path is not achieved.

### 2026-08-03 18:11:22  #Trading Economics #Economy #Peru #Inflation Rate #Importance 0

Peru Annual Inflation at 2023-Highs — The annual inflation rate in Peru's capital, Lima, edged up to 4.07% in July 2026 from 4.01% in the previous month, reaching its highest level since October 2023. The increase was mainly driven by faster price growth for food (3.5% vs 3.3%), education (3.6% vs 3.4%), furnishings and household equipment (3.2% vs 3.0%), housing and utilities (1.3% vs 1.2%), recreation and culture (2.2% vs 2.0%), clothing and footwear (1.5% vs 1.4%), and health (1.3% vs 1.2%). Meanwhile, deflation for communication and information eased (-2.6% vs -4.6%), while inflation for restaurants and hotels remained unchanged at 4.2%. In contrast, transport inflation moderated slightly (14.3% vs 14.6%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 0.29%, accelerating from a 0.23% increase in June.

【China rolls out policy package to spur consumption by improving consumer environment】Local authorities are deploying measures to convert inbound tourism into retail demand: expanding the 240-hour transit visa-free policy, accelerating rollout of immediate departure tax refunds, broadening merchant acceptance of foreign bank cards and mobile payments, and adding multilingual shopping guides in key districts. Beijing’s Xiushui Street operator said 1H footfall rose sharply, recording over 4.3 mln inbound visitors and about 2.0 bln yuan in inbound sales in 1H, up 550% and 350% YoY respectively. Nationally, inbound foreign arrivals reached 22.914 mln in 1H 2026, +20.4% YoY; visa-free arrivals were 17.815 mln (77.7% share), +30.6% YoY. Tourism flows are increasingly translating into consumption gains.

### 2026-08-03 18:03:50  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Africa #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 0

South Africa's Bond Yield Falls — South Africa's 10-year government bond yield fell to around 8.65%, its lowest level since mid-July, as declining oil prices eased inflation concerns and dampened expectations of further interest rate hikes. Crude prices dropped after US President Donald Trump announced that fresh talks with Iran would begin on Monday, easing fears of a broader Middle East conflict and boosting global risk sentiment. On the domestic front, July PMI data showed South Africa's manufacturing sector contracted further, weighed down by weak export demand and lingering geopolitical uncertainty. Last month, the South African Reserve Bank unexpectedly left its benchmark interest rate unchanged, defying expectations of a 25-basis-point increase. Meanwhile, annual inflation accelerated to 5.0% in June, the highest in two years, while core inflation rose to 4.1%, its strongest reading since September 2024. Both measures remain above the upper end of the SARB's 3% ±1 percentage point target range.

### 2026-08-03 17:54:55  #Trading Economics #Economy #Pakistan #Inflation Rate #Importance 0

Pakistan Annual Inflation Eases in July — The annual inflation rate in Pakistan eased to 9.2% in July 2026, from 11.1% in the previous month, remaining well above the State Bank of Pakistan's 5%-7% target range. The slowdown was mainly driven by softer inflation for housing and utilities (7.1% vs 15.5%) and transport (15.1% vs 25.7%), while clothing and footwear inflation edged lower (9.2% vs 9.3%). In contrast, food inflation accelerated to 10.6% from 9.4%, as prices of non-perishable food items remained elevated (11.6% vs 10.2%). Additional upward pressure came from restaurants and hotels (5.7% vs 5.4%), education (9.0% vs 8.3%), health (7.8% vs 7.6%), and communication, where inflation surged to 13.6% from 0.9%. On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 1.2% in July, rebounding from a 0.3% decline in June.

【藥明康德：全面上調2026年全年業績指引 預計2026年整體收入由513-530億元上調至585-605億元】藥明康德公告，得益於多個客戶產品的更大成功，以及公司獨特的CRDMO模式和卓越的管理與執行，公司全面上調2026年全年業績指引。預計2026年公司整體收入由人民幣513-530億元上調至人民幣585-605億元，持續經營業務收入同比增速由18%-22%上調至35%-39%。公司將繼續推動高質量增長，穩步釋放規模效益、精進運營管理能力，高效前置管理新建產能爬坡及匯率波動等挑戰。公司有信心在2026年保持穩定且有韌性的經調整非《國際財務報告準則》之歸母淨利潤率水平。為更好滿足日益增長的客戶需求，公司進一步加速全球產能佈局，並提前啟動常州新基地建設，預計2026年資本開支由人民幣65-75億元上調至人民幣75-85億元。隨著業務增長及效率提升，預計2026年經調整自由現金流由人民幣105-115億元上調至人民幣135-145億元。

### 2026-08-03 17:42:08  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Africa #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

South Africa Factory Activity Contracts Again: Absa — South Africa’s seasonally adjusted Absa Purchasing Managers’ Index (PMI) dropped to 46.8 in July 2026 from 47.3 in June, contracting for a second consecutive month. Domestic demand and production continued to recover, with shorter supplier delivery times. Inventories declined further as firms remain uncertain that strong demand will persist, although some firms could be delaying purchases expecting lower input costs in the future. Input cost pressures remain elevated relative to the pre-war period, with increases in diesel prices expected to put renewed pressures on costs. Nevertheless, peak inflationary pressures have likely passed, barring any further upsurge in global energy costs. Looking ahead, firms expect business conditions to weaken as renewed tensions in the Middle East push up oil prices, making firms questions the durability of recent improvements in activity.

【羅馬尼亞在多瑙河爆破向核電站引水】今天（8月3日），羅馬尼亞海軍部隊繼續在多瑙河的巴拉水道河段實施可控爆破，以將水流引向切爾納沃德核電站——此次爆破旨在將巴拉水道的水流匯入多瑙河舊河道，從而抬升水位。羅馬尼亞總理7月31日表示，受多瑙河水位跌至歷史低點影響，切爾納沃德核電站的兩座核反應堆先後於7月28日和30日停運，這是該核電站兩座反應堆首次因多瑙河水位過低同時處於停運狀態。核反應堆停運導致發電量下降，羅馬尼亞為此宣佈全境進入為期一個月的緊急狀態，緊急狀態將覆蓋整個8月。（CCTV國際時訊）

Turkey's Trade Deficit Widens in July — Turkey's trade deficit widened to $7.37 billion in July 2026 from $6.46 billion a year earlier, according to preliminary data, as import growth continued to outpace exports. Imports increased 5.2% year-on-year to $32.99 billion, while exports rose 2.9% to $25.62 billion. In the first seven months of 2026, the country's cumulative trade deficit reached $60.51 billion. During the period, imports climbed 4.7% to $222.11 billion, while exports increased 3.4% to $161.60 billion.

### 2026-08-03 17:21:23  #Trading Economics #Economy #Nigeria #Composite PMI #Importance 1

Nigeria Private Sector Growth Softens in July — The Stanbic IBTC Bank Nigeria PMI eased to 52.5 in July 2026 from 53.4 in June, signalling a sixth consecutive monthly improvement in private sector operating conditions. New orders continued to rise, supported by product launches, competitive pricing, and stronger customer demand. Business activity increased further, led by gains in agriculture and manufacturing, while growth in services and retail moderated. Employment and purchasing activity expanded as firms responded to higher workloads, although backlogs rose slightly due to logistical challenges despite improved supplier performance. Inflationary pressures softened, with both input costs and output prices rising at slower rates, while staff costs increased modestly. Agriculture recorded the strongest increase in selling prices, whereas services posted the weakest inflation. Firms remained optimistic about output over the coming year, although confidence eased from June's one-year high.

### 2026-08-03 17:11:24  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Aluminum Rises to Near 6-Week High — Aluminum futures in the UK rose above $3,210 per tonne, the highest level in nearly six weeks, amid ongoing supply constraints and slower production. Output outside China fell 6.7% year-on-year in July, mainly due to reduced operating rates at several Middle Eastern smelters. These pressures were compounded by China’s 45 million-ton production cap, which is expected to become more restrictive this year. Geopolitical tensions have also prompted consumers to draw down exchange inventories, with LME stocks falling to their lowest level this century. In addition, Alcoa Corp. cut its production forecast following operational issues at an Australian refinery. However, some of these supply concerns could be offset by anticipated capacity restarts and expansion progress among producers, including the restart of the Slovalco smelter in Slovakia in Q4 of 2026 and Metals’ Missouri smelter by year-end. Emirates Global Aluminium also continued restoring production at its Al Taweelah facility.

### 2026-08-03 17:08:49  #Trading Economics #Economy #Cyprus #Retail Sales YoY #Importance 1

Cyprus Retail Trade Growth Eases in June — Retail sales in Cyprus rose 5.5% year-on-year in June 2026, easing from a 7.5% increase in May. The slowdown mainly reflected weaker growth in food, beverages and tobacco in non-specialised stores (2.1% vs 7.4%), clothing and footwear (17.1% vs 19.4%), and cultural and recreation goods (10.1% vs 11.3%). Sales also continued to decline for automotive fuel (-3.5% vs -3.8%) and food, beverages and tobacco in specialised stores (-0.5% vs 1.2%). Meanwhile, growth accelerated for retail sales not in stores (20.5% vs 3.0%), information and communication equipment (20.1% vs 17.6%), other household equipment (14.1% vs 13.3%), and pharmaceuticals, orthopedic goods, cosmetics (5.6% vs 4.0%). Excluding automotive fuel, retail trade rose 6.4%, easing from 8.7% in May. In the first half of 2026, retail sales increased 5.8% compared with the same period a year earlier.

### 2026-08-03 17:03:53  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

UK Manufacturing Growth Eases to 4-Month Low — The S&P Global UK Manufacturing PMI eased to 51.9 in July 2026, a four-month low, from 52.5 in June and below the preliminary estimate of 52.8. Despite the decline, the index remained above the 50 mark for a ninth consecutive month, signaling continued expansion in the sector. Manufacturing output rose for a fourth straight month, accelerating to its fastest pace in nearly two years. New orders increased for an eighth consecutive month, while export sales grew for a seventh month. However, business optimism slipped to a three-month low as firms remained cautious about global trade tensions, tax changes, and regulatory uncertainty. Employment continued to rise but at the slowest pace of the current recovery, reflecting ongoing caution over hiring. Meanwhile, input cost and output price inflation eased further as supply chain pressures moderated, while purchasing activity and inventories declined amid softer demand for inputs and improved supplier delivery times.

### 2026-08-03 16:57:51  #公司 #觀點

【中信證券： 商業健康險開啟“換擋升級”，預計2030年貢獻3000億支付增量】中信證券釋出研報稱，基本醫保收支邊界日益收緊促使商業健康險加速成為核心增量支付方，同時創新藥管線的密集兌現也強勢倒逼商保支付體系全面擴容。在此趨勢下，我國商保將沿著“惠民保普及至中高階醫療險滲透”的路徑發展，預計至2030年有望為醫療健康產業貢獻約3000億元的支付增量，其中創新藥械的賠付佔比將大幅提升。面對這一廣闊的市場空間，具備資料穿透力與藥險資源整合能力的綜合服務平臺型公司將脫穎而出，成為引領行業演進的關鍵樞紐。基於上述產業趨勢，投資組合可重點聚焦四大主線：1）創新藥械——商業健康險目錄後續推出有望覆蓋創新藥械，創新藥械有望受益於商業健康險帶來的增量支付額以及估值體系提升；2）平臺型公司賦能藥險協同，開啟支付體系“換擋升級”新週期；3）民營醫療終端——醫療服務、藥店有望迎來新支付方，開啟未來業績成長空間，估值中樞有望上移；4）醫藥流通——獨佔高值創新藥首發通道，綜合供應鏈巨頭迎價值重估

【Tianjin grid peak load tops 20 GW for first time, sets record】Sustained hot, humid weather pushed Tianjin power grid peak load to 20.949 GW at 13:32 on Aug. 3, the first exceedance of 20 GW and a new record — up 1.579 GW (8.15%) from last year’s peak of 19.37 GW. Tianjin power authorities have deployed precise load forecasting, dynamically optimized grid operations, expanded interprovincial power purchases to increase external inflows, coordinated local generation to keep conventional units running at full stable output, and are rolling-monitoring renewables and strengthening plant operations and grid services to boost reliable renewable output during peak periods.

### 2026-08-03 16:44:52  #MKT News #Important #Commodities #Industrial Metals #Market Regions #Emerging Markets

【China Iron and Steel Association to tighten enforcement of steel export licensing】China Iron and Steel Association said H1 2026 steel exports fell overall with stable prices; steel billet shipments rose sharply and 12 preliminary overseas investigations into Chinese steel were filed. It warned external constraints will tighten in H2 as the EU’s new steel safeguard cuts quotas and introduces a smelting-and-casting origin rule, and cited broader geopolitical disruption that will complicate exports. The association said it will strictly implement the steel export licensing system, prioritizing higher-end products, stabilizing nearby markets and tightening supervision. It called for stronger industry self-discipline, optimization of export structure, deeper focus on neighboring and emerging markets, proactive responses to trade frictions and adaptation to international rules to drive a shift toward higher-end, greener exports and ensure orderly development.

The plan mandates region‑appropriate full‑load denitrification (SCR) retrofits for coal units and implementation of a batch of cross‑generation upgrade projects at the ~600 MW class. In eligible regions it supports construction of zero‑carbon/low‑carbon fuel co‑firing and carbon capture, utilization and storage (CCUS) projects. Regulators are to issue coal power–new energy integration policies, prioritise coal–renewable integration pilots, retrofit coal units for enhanced regulation/flexibility, and push system coupling and integrated dispatch to lower coal‑fired generation.

【China State Planner, National Energy Administration issue plan to improve rules for new power-market participants】China State Planner and the National Energy Administration issued the 15th Five-Year Plan for New Power System Construction, proposing a '1+N' basic-rule framework for the power market and tighter alignment of national and local rules. The plan aims to broaden participation by new market entities, normalize cross-grid trading and gradually reduce proxy (agency) power procurement. It calls for improved ancillary services markets and exploration of a capacity market, deeper electricity price reform and market-based pricing for hydro, nuclear and gas generation. The plan proposes exploring two-part or single-capacity transmission pricing for cross-provincial special projects, implementing price mechanisms to support nearby consumption of renewables, refining transmission-and-distribution tariff incentives and constraints to steer grid investment, studying pricing for offshore wind evacuation, and optimizing residential tiered tariffs while promoting residential time-of-use pricing.

It aims to enable high‑level integration and utilization of renewables—accommodating more than 2.8 billion kW (≈2,800 GW) of new energy capacity—and to build charging infrastructure to support over 110 million electric vehicles. Institutional reforms include more complete system mechanisms and the basic establishment of a nationwide unified electricity market.

### 2026-08-03 16:28:18  #Trading Economics #Economy #Euro Area #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

Eurozone Manufacturing Growth Strengthens to Highest Since April — The S&P Global Eurozone Manufacturing PMI rose to 51.9 in July 2026 from 51.4 in June, broadly matching the preliminary estimate of 52.0 and signaling the strongest improvement in factory conditions since April. Manufacturing output expanded at its fastest pace since March 2022, supported by the completion of backlogged orders and a modest increase in new business. However, demand remained subdued, with new orders rising only marginally and export orders declining again. Backlogs of work fell for a third consecutive month, recording the sharpest depletion since January, while manufacturers continued to cut jobs and reduce purchasing activity. Cost pressures remained elevated but eased to a five-month low, allowing factory gate price inflation to soften to its weakest level since March. Meanwhile, business confidence improved to its highest level since February, although it remained below its long-run average.

【Bank of Japan warns AI demand may boost inflation】The Bank of Japan said on Monday global AI-related demand could place sustained upward pressure on Japanese inflation, a development that could reinforce the case for its recent policy tightening. It noted that while AI adoption should raise productivity over the medium to long term and exert downward pressure on prices as firms and workers adjust, in the short term AI-driven investment could lift demand and push prices higher, potentially outweighing productivity gains. The BOJ flagged rising global producer prices partly driven by Middle East-related oil moves and partly by a positive global demand shock for AI-related goods, and said those spillovers, combined with import-cost increases from yen depreciation, could keep domestic inflation elevated for some time.

### 2026-08-03 16:23:32  #Trading Economics #Economy #Kazakhstan #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Kazakhstan Inflation Rate Hits Over 1-Year Low — The annual inflation rate in Kazakhstan eased to 10.2% in July 2026 from 10.3% in June, marking the lowest reading since March 2025. Food inflation slowed to 10.1% from 10.4%, mainly reflecting deflation in fruits, nuts, and berries (-0.8% vs 1.5%) and slower price growth for meat and poultry (15.8% vs 17.6%) and eggs (14.2% vs 15.3%). Meanwhile, inflation for non-food products held steady at 11.7%, with detergent prices unchanged at 11.1%, while inflation for paid services edged up to 9.2% from 9.0%, driven by faster price growth in housing and utilities (5.7% vs 5.1%), rental property (14.6% vs 13.9%), and the maintenance, repair, and security of residential premises (12.5% vs 11.5%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices eased slightly to 0.6% in July from a 0.8% increase in June and marking the slowest monthly inflation in four months.

### 2026-08-03 16:16:56  #Trading Economics #Economy #Thailand #Business Confidence #Importance 1

Thailand Business Morale at 4-Month High — Thailand’s Business Sentiment Index rose to 46.7 in July 2026 from 46.1 in June, the highest reading in four months.The improvement was driven mainly by stronger sentiment in the non-manufacturing sector, supported by lower fuel costs and increased flight services, while gains were partly offset by weaker confidence in manufacturing amid concerns over US import tariffs. Sentiment improved for business performance (50.0 vs 49.3) and investment (51.2 vs 50.7), while expectations for production (48.6 vs 51.9), employment (48.9 vs 50.2), and total order books (47.5 vs 48.1) turned less optimistic. Meanwhile, manufacturing confidence weakened, led by lower order books in the home electrical appliance industry. High production costs remained the top business constraint for the fifth straight month, while inflation expectations for the next 12 months eased slightly to 2.6%. Moreover, the three-month expected Business Sentiment Index rose to 50.7 from 48.0.

### 2026-08-03 16:14:06  #Trading Economics #Economy #Switzerland #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

Swiss Factory Activity Hits 5-Month Low — Switzerland’s procure.ch–UBS Manufacturing PMI fell to 53.2 in July 2026 from 54.3 in the previous month, falling short of market expectations of 55. The reading marked the weakest level since February, as growth momentum softened across key components, with production expansion slowing to 54.2 from 57.0 in June and order books edging down to 53.2 from 53.5. Purchasing activity also weakened, with purchasing volumes falling into contraction territory at 48.1 from 50.9 and purchasing inventories declining to 47.5 from 50.4. Moreover, sales stocks (46.8 vs 48.1) and employment (47.7 vs 48.9) continued to deteriorate. Supplier delivery times lengthened further (62.9 vs 60.9), indicating rising delivery delays. On the price front, cost pressures intensified, with the index jumping to 74.2 in July, from 72.8 in the previous month.

### 2026-08-03 16:12:39  #Trading Economics #Economy #Greece #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

Greece Factory Activity Expands in July — The S&P Global Greece Manufacturing PMI rose to 54.3 in July 2026 from 53.8 in June, signalling a solid improvement in operating conditions. Output expanded at the fastest pace in five months, supported by sustained client demand and further growth in new orders. Export orders also increased for the first time in six months and at the fastest rate since April 2025. Employment rose at the sharpest pace since November 2025, allowing firms to keep up with incoming work as backlogs declined for a second month. Input costs continued to increase, driven by higher material and energy prices, although inflationary pressures eased. Output price inflation also softened to a four-month low. Pre-production inventories fell for a fourth consecutive month amid longer supplier lead times caused by shipping delays and shortages. Firms remained optimistic about production over the coming year, although confidence eased slightly.

### 2026-08-03 16:09:37  #MKT News #Brent #Impact mixed

【Overview: Iran-Oman talks on the Strait of Hormuz】Oman last week proposed a Malacca Strait‑style joint regional management mechanism for navigation safety in the Strait of Hormuz, funded by voluntary levies on shipping companies; Iran rejected the proposal. Oman also proposed splitting the channel evenly between Iranian and Omani territorial waters so vessels enter on one side and exit on the other; Iran rejected that plan and counter‑proposed that Iran manage its side while Oman manage only part of the opposite channel — a structure that would effectively allow Iran to control traffic on both inbound and outbound lanes. Iranian foreign minister Araghchi said talks with Oman are in their final stages to design a route that respects both countries' sovereignty; foreign ministry spokesman Baghaei said Tehran seeks an acceptable route (neither the northern nor southern lane) to safeguard Iran’s national interests and security and is working with Oman to open a temporary security corridor. Oman has not publicly responded.

### 2026-08-03 16:08:12  #MKT News #Market Regions #Europe & UK #EURUSD #Impact bearish #DXY #Impact bullish

【ECB says Iran war hit euro-area consumption, April growth slows to ~2.5%】The ECB said euro-area household consumption plunged in the initial weeks after the outbreak of the Iran war as consumer confidence collapsed, and warned a renewed escalation could again curb spending. In its economic bulletin the central bank said April consumption fell to roughly 2.5% YoY — about half the pace seen since mid-2024 when nominal consumption averaged 3–4% — and that the April drop was roughly twice the decline implied by historical-trend forecasts and comparable to the hit after the Russia‑Ukraine outbreak in early 2022. The slowdown was driven by higher-income households cutting discretionary spending; the ECB said this pattern points to households postponing purchases rather than being income-constrained.

【摩根大通策略師：人工智慧難以在下半年推動股市上漲】摩根大通策略師表示，隨著市場行情擴散，科技股及人工智慧相關個股不太可能成為下半年市場收益的核心驅動力。由米斯拉夫・馬泰伊卡牽頭的策略團隊稱，趨勢交易板塊的拋售行情大機率接近尾聲，半導體股票已臨近超賣區間。團隊在研報中寫道：“每股收益增長動能依舊向上，這將助力該板塊企穩。”儘管如此，策略師預計市場行情將持續擴散，並重申建議：因地緣政治緊張引發股價回撥時逢低買入。團隊同時預期美聯儲將採取最大限度寬鬆政策，推動債券收益率下行或美元走弱，兩種情形均會利好股市。

### 2026-08-03 16:05:06  #MKT News #Market Regions #Europe & UK #EURUSD #Impact bullish

【Eurozone July manufacturing PMI rises to 51.9 as backlogs drive output gains】Eurozone manufacturing PMI rose to 51.9 in July from 51.4 in June, S&P Global said, slightly below the earlier flash 52.0. The output sub-index climbed to 52.9 from 51.7, its highest since March 2022. S&P Global noted the improvement largely reflects firms digesting backlogs rather than a pickup in end-market demand. Disruption from the Middle East conflict and sharply higher energy costs are adding operating pressure on manufacturers. S&P Global Market Intelligence chief business economist Chris Williamson said the sector is experiencing a degree of summer acceleration but warned this performance may prove hard to sustain and momentum could weaken into autumn.

---

### 2026-08-03 16:05:00  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

Germany Manufacturing Sees Strong Start to Q3 — The S&P Global Germany Manufacturing PMI was confirmed at 52.2 in July 2026, the highest in four months, compared to 50.3 in June, signalling an uptick in manufacturing performance. "Germany's manufacturing sector made an impressive start to the third quarter, with a notable improvement in export sales helping drive the strongest production growth in nearly four-and-a-half years. An easing of cost inflation to the weakest seen since the outbreak of the Middle East war, primarily linked to the drop in oil prices through June and into early July, was likely a supporting factor too", according to Phil Smith, Economics Associate Director at S&P Global Market Intelligence. However, business expectations remain subdued in the manufacturing sector, staying below the level seen prior to the outbreak of the conflict. Supply chain disruption remains an ongoing risk, with delays having worsened again in July due in part to the bottlenecks forming in the global electronics industry.

### 2026-08-03 16:04:00  #Trading Economics #Markets #United Kingdom #Stock Market #Importance 1

FTSE 100 Trades Cautiously on Monday — The FTSE 100 fluctuated on Monday, with gains capped by weakness in energy stocks and AstraZeneca. Investor sentiment improved after US President Donald Trump said fresh talks with Iran would begin today, raising hopes of easing geopolitical tensions. Optimism surrounding a potential agreement that could reopen the Strait of Hormuz pushed crude oil prices lower, weighing on oil majors Shell and BP, which both fell more than 2%. AstraZeneca dropped over 6% after reports that the pharmaceutical group had held discussions with US drugmaker Bristol Myers Squibb about a possible merger. In contrast, homebuilders Persimmon and Barratt Redrow gained more than 4% as markets scaled back expectations for further Bank of England rate hikes. Banks also advanced, while aerospace engineer Rolls-Royce rose around 3.8%.

### 2026-08-03 15:57:55  #Trading Economics #Economy #France #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

France Manufacturing Sector Contracts in July — The S&P Global France Manufacturing PMI fell to 49.8 in July 2026 from 51.2 in June, signaling a renewed deterioration in factory activity after the preliminary estimate had pointed to stagnation. Production and new orders declined at faster rates as demand weakened amid price pressures, subdued client confidence, and the Middle East conflict, while export orders posted their steepest decline in a year. Manufacturers cut purchasing activity at the fastest pace since March and continued reducing input and finished goods inventories. Backlogs of work declined for only the second time this year, although employment rose at the fastest pace since December. Supply-chain pressures persisted, with delivery delays remaining elevated due to shortages of electronic components and chemical products. Meanwhile, input cost inflation eased to a four-month low, while output price inflation moderated only slightly. Business confidence improved on hopes of stronger sales conditions in the year ahead.

### 2026-08-03 15:57:22  #Trading Economics #Economy #Spain #Tourist Arrivals #Importance 1

Spain Tourist Arrivals Continue to Rise — Spain received 9.746 million international tourists in June 2026, up 2.9% yoy and marking a new record for the month. The UK remained the largest source market, with nearly 2.3 million visitors, an increase of 6.8%. By contrast, arrivals from Germany fell 5.9% to 1.2 million, while those from France declined 1.7% to 1.0 million. The number of tourists choosing market-based accommodation as their primary lodging option edged down 0.6%. Within this category, hotel stays decreased 1.7%, while demand for rental accommodation rose 10.1%. Meanwhile, the use of non-market accommodation, including stays with friends or relatives and in privately owned properties, jumped 26.8%. In the first half of 2026, Spain received around 46.6 million international tourists, up 4.6% from the same period a year earlier. The UK remained the largest source market, with more than 9.3 million visitors (+4.3%), followed by Germany with 5.7 million (-1.1%) and France with more than 5.6 million (+1.0%).

### 2026-08-03 15:55:11  #Trading Economics #Economy #Italy #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 2

Italy Manufacturing Growth Slows to Four-Month Low — The S&P Global Italy Manufacturing PMI fell to 51.3 in July 2026 from 52.2 in June, missing market expectations of 52.3 and marking its lowest reading in four months. The slowdown reflected fading support from earlier stockpiling linked to the Iran conflict, as new orders declined again amid subdued demand, with firms citing geopolitical uncertainty and inflationary pressures. Export orders also fell for the first time in five months. Manufacturing output expanded at a softer pace, while longer supplier delivery times provided a positive contribution to the headline index despite reflecting ongoing supply disruptions. Backlogs of work recorded their sharpest decline of the year, and both employment and purchasing activity weakened. At the same time, input cost pressures eased, while business confidence deteriorated.

India 10Y Yield Rises as Index Inclusion Delayed — The yield on India’s 10-year G-Sec hovered around 6.84%, edging higher after losses in the previous session as investor sentiment remained subdued following Bloomberg's decision to defer the inclusion of Indian government bonds in its Global Aggregate Index. The decision disappointed investors who had largely viewed index inclusion as a near certainty, triggering foreign sales of nearly $600 million in bonds under the Fully Accessible Route over the last six sessions, including the biggest weekly outflow in four months. Still, overseas investors remained net buyers of $3.9 billion in FAR bonds between June 1 and July 31. Investors also awaited this week's Reserve Bank of India policy meeting, where economists expect the central bank to keep interest rates unchanged, while closely monitoring its assessment of inflation risks from elevated oil prices and weak monsoon rainfall.

### 2026-08-03 15:54:20  #Trading Economics #Economy #Czech Republic #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

Czechia Factory Growth Slows in July — The Czech Manufacturing PMI fell to 52.2 in July 2026 from 53.9 in June, which marked the highest level since April 2022. The latest reading was the joint-softest since February (alongside May 2026), reflecting slower growth in both output and new orders compared with June. Still, new orders continued to be supported by foreign demand, with new export sales rising at the fastest pace since August 2021. However, pressure on balance sheets reportedly led firms to limit increases in input buying and pre-production inventories, while also contributing to a renewed decline in employment. Backlogs of work continued to accumulate at a solid pace as supply chain pressures persisted, although their severity eased. On the cost front, both input cost and output price inflation moderated in July but remained historically elevated, as the prices of key inputs, including metals and chemicals, continued to rise sharply. Business confidence fell to a three-month low but remained historically elevated.

【Russia boosts Black Sea shipping protection, develops alternative cargo routes】Russia said on Monday it is strengthening protection for vessels in the Azov–Black Sea region and developing alternative cargo routes following a recent escalation in maritime attacks between Russia and Ukraine. The transport ministry said hostile drone strikes on ships had heightened tensions in the Sea of Azov, prompting formation of a special task force to map new transport routes and divert some cargo to other modes. Several terminal operators have told authorities they can accept additional volumes within their operational capacity and accelerate cargo handling and shipment. The ministry said it will coordinate additional measures with the defence ministry to secure navigation and protect merchant vessels in the Azov–Black Sea area. Russia is the world’s largest wheat exporter; Ukraine is also a major agricultural exporter.

French Stocks Hit Over 5-Month High — The CAC 40 climbed 1% to 8,591 on the first trading day of August, reaching its highest level since late February, as investors embraced renewed hopes for diplomacy after US President Donald Trump announced that talks with Iran would resume following his decision to halt a planned strike. The easing geopolitical tensions drove oil prices lower and encouraged markets to scale back bets on additional ECB tightening, although a September hike remains widely expected. Market optimism was further supported by a fresh batch of corporate earnings reports and company updates. Luxury stocks advanced, with LVMH (1.4%), Hermès (1.2%) and Kering (0.2%) gaining after Kering reported 1% first-half revenue growth as Gucci’s decline narrowed. Industrial heavyweights also supported the index, including Schneider Electric (1.4%), Airbus (3%) and Safran (2.8%), following Airbus’ strong first-half performance and progress toward resolving its Pratt & Whitney dispute.

【多地加碼購車補貼，重慶首次將“電驢”納入購新政策範圍】多地為促進汽車消費正在陸續推出購車消費補貼。8月1日，西安市釋出汽車消費補貼政策，個人消費者購買納入《減免車輛購置稅的新能源汽車車型目錄》的新能源乘用車，按照購車發票價格的2%給予補貼，最高補貼1萬元。同時，西安市對二手車也有補貼。其中，濟南市商務局釋出公告稱，自8月1日起，消費者申領汽車報廢更新或置換更新補貼，無須提前領取資格，可直接透過微信、支付寶相關小程式提交材料。青島市商務局明確，8月汽車報廢更新和置換更新補貼配額合計2.5億元，按照“先到先得”原則分配。重慶則是自8月1日起調整了汽車購新補貼金額梯度，並首次新增實施摩托車及電動腳踏車購新補貼政策，具體標準為：個人消費者報廢符合條件的舊車併購買新能源乘用車新車的，按新車銷售價格的12%給予補貼，補貼金額最高2萬元；購買燃油乘用車新車的，按新車銷售價格的10%給予補貼，補貼金額最高1.5萬元；個人購買電動腳踏車、兩輪電動摩托車（含兩輪電動輕便摩托車）、兩輪燃油摩托車（含兩輪燃油輕便摩托車）新車，按車價金額的10%予以補貼，單車最高補貼1500元。

【國家文物局：不減損價值前提下，支援對未定級民居類文物建築進行適應性改造】國家文物局印發《未定級民居類文物建築修繕審批工作指引（試行）》提出，為滿足人民群眾基本生活需求，在不減損文物價值、不影響文物安全的前提下，支援對未定級民居類文物建築進行必要的適應性改造。可包括：安裝空調、設定或升級廚衛設施、幹槎瓦屋面增設防水構造、改善門窗隔熱效能、改善地面隔潮效能、改善牆體隔音效能、不涉及原歷史裝修的粉刷飾面、提升消防效能、改造升級水電暖等基礎設施裝置。適應性改造應儘可能與修繕工程或簡易修繕工程一併設計、施工。如單獨實施，縣級文物行政部門應會同鄉鎮人民政府（街道）、村（社群），對其進行必要指導和監督，選擇恰當的位置、技術路徑和材料，不應套用當代建築規範，避免對文物造成負面影響。必要時可赴現場踏勘協商。

European Stocks Kick Off August Higher — European stocks started August on a positive note, with the STOXX 50 rising 0.9% and the STOXX 600 gaining 0.3%, as investors prepared for a fresh round of corporate earnings and welcomed lower oil prices after US President Donald Trump said new US-Iran talks would begin on Monday. Airlines led the gains, with Lufthansa (+3.8%) and Ryanair (+3.3%) among the top performers. Airbus (+3.2%), SAP (+3.7%), and Safran (+3.2%) also advanced, while Worldline surged nearly 7% to top the STOXX 600, extending Friday's strong rally. On the downside, AstraZeneca tumbled more than 6% following reports that it was in merger talks with Bristol Myers Squibb on a potential $400 billion deal. Energy stocks also came under pressure as oil prices declined, with Eni (-2.2%), TotalEnergies (-1.8%), and Shell (-1.1%) all trading lower.

### 2026-08-03 15:45:13  #Trading Economics #Economy #Turkey #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

Turkey Manufacturing Downturn Eases — The Istanbul Chamber of Industry Türkiye Manufacturing PMI rose to 47.7 in July from 47.1 in June, but remained below the 50.0 threshold and signaled a deterioration in manufacturing conditions for the 28th consecutive month. New orders declined further amid subdued market conditions and price pressures, while international demand also weakened due to the war in the Middle East. Output contracted for a second straight month, prompting firms to reduce employment, purchasing activity, and inventories. Meanwhile, supplier delivery times lengthened at the slowest pace in nine months as softer input demand eased supply chain pressures, although delays in sea transportation persisted. On prices, input cost inflation slowed for the third consecutive month to its weakest since November 2025 despite higher oil and raw material costs, while selling price inflation eased to its lowest level of 2026 so far.

【Strategists: Yen appreciation could lift Asian currencies】Citigroup and Barclays strategists said yen appreciation could strengthen Asian currencies with high yen correlation, naming the South Korean won, Singapore dollar and Thai baht as the most likely beneficiaries. Japan and the United States previously intervened in FX markets to support the yen after it fell to multidecade lows. As of Monday the yen had risen over 4% vs the dollar in the past three trading days; the won, baht and Philippine peso each gained at least 0.7% over the same period. Barclays said recent USD/JPY intervention may have been coordinated and its spillovers to Asian FX could exceed historical correlations, potentially further lifting yen‑sensitive Asian currencies in the near term, particularly if the dollar weakens. Barclays also flagged that Fed chair Kevin Warsh’s relatively dovish press conference last week helped push the dollar lower, providing additional support for Asian currencies.

【港交所推出全球首個離岸國債期貨，補齊外資跨境對沖工具短板】8月3日，香港交易所正式推出全球首個離岸中國國債期貨產品——5年期中國國債期貨，首批上市的9月及12月兩個季月合約同步開始交易。這是全球離岸市場首隻以中國國債為標的的期貨產品，全程以人民幣計價、交易及結算，同時支援香港公眾假期交易。在此之前，境外投資者持有人民幣債券資產時，缺乏便捷的離岸對沖工具，主要依靠中金所在岸國債期貨或互換通等渠道管理利率風險，但兩者均存在准入門檻較高或操作不夠便捷的侷限。產品上市首年，港交所將對所有市場參與者實行交易手續費減半優惠，單張合約標準手續費為人民幣5元，優惠期內減至人民幣2.5元。每張合約名義本金為人民幣50萬元，港交所對經紀商收取的保證金比例為1.2%，單張合約保證金約為人民幣6000元，終端投資者的實際保證金由各家券商自主設定。（21財經）

【韓國大盤股獲利回吐，資金輪動KOSDAQ強勢反彈】韓國KOSPI指數與韓國創業板指（KOSDAQ）今天走勢出現明顯分化。在前一交易日大幅上漲後，KOSPI進入調整階段，而KOSDAQ盤中一度上漲超過4%，並觸發買入側車（程式化交易暫停）機制。根據韓國交易所資料，當天KOSPI市場中，個人投資者淨買入6.4338萬億韓元，而外國投資者和機構投資者分別淨賣出3.8708萬億韓元和2.66萬億韓元。在KOSDAQ市場，個人投資者和機構投資者分別淨買入598億韓元和1629億韓元，而外國投資者淨賣出2305億韓元。上月31日，三星電子和SK海力士股價分別暴漲26.81%和29.95%。大信證券研究員李京敏分析稱：“由於股價快速上漲，外國投資者主要針對這兩隻股票進行獲利了結，導致市場反轉。”今天，三星電子和SK海力士均收跌約8%。隨著資金從半導體轉向製藥、生物技術和機器人等板塊，韓國創業板保持強勢，Peptron股價觸及漲停，Alteogen、LigaChem Biosciences、ABL Bio和Sam Chun Dang Pharm等公司股價也走強。

### 2026-08-03 15:34:06  #Trading Economics #Economy #Pakistan #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

Pakistan Wholesale Inflation at 4-Month Low — Wholesale price inflation in Pakistan eased to 9.43% year-on-year in July 2026 from 10.69% in the previous month, marking the lowest reading since March. Costs declined for ores, minerals, electricity, gas, and water (-0.39% vs 5.69% in June), while price growth moderated for other transportable goods (20.44% vs 27.96%). Meanwhile, inflation accelerated for agriculture, forestry, and fishery (7.61% vs 1.80%), food, beverages, tobacco, textiles, and leather products (7.40% vs 7.14%), driven mainly by food products, beverages, and tobacco (9.40% vs 9.0%). Price growth also picked up for metal products, machinery, and equipment (6.19% vs 5.67%). On a monthly basis, wholesale prices were unchanged in July after falling 1.21% in the previous month.

### 2026-08-03 15:29:58  #Trading Economics #Economy #Spain #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 2

Spain Manufacturing Activity Remains Subdued — The S&P Spain Manufacturing PMI rose to 50.2 in July 2026 from 49.7 in June, hovering just above the expansion threshold. Despite the slight uptick, underlying performance remained weak, as both output and new orders continued to decline. Ongoing Middle East conflict disruptions caused persistent shipping delays, supply chain bottlenecks, and elevated energy and transport costs. However, overall input and output price inflation cooled to a five-month low. In response to sluggish market demand, manufacturers aggressively drew down existing input and finished goods inventories while cutting purchasing activity at the sharpest rate in over a year. Employment contracted marginally for the eleventh consecutive month due to unreplaced staff departures and hiring hesitations. Nevertheless, broader business sentiment rebounded to its highest level since February, driven by long-term growth plans and expectations of an eventual market recovery.

### 2026-08-03 15:26:22  #Trading Economics #Economy #Turkey #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

Turkey Producer Inflation Slows to 5-Month Low — The annual producer inflation rate in Turkey eased to 27.83% in July 2026 from 28.09% in the previous month. It was the softest rise since February, driven by smaller price increases for mining and quarrying (41.81% vs 53.50% in June), manufacturing (28.96% vs 29.65%), and water supply (28.85% vs 29.26%). On the other hand, producer inflation picked up for electricity, gas, steam and air conditioning (11.69% vs 4.12%). By industrial groupings, price growth moderated for intermediate goods (26.79% vs 27.89%), durable goods (26.57%), and capital goods (21.28% vs 23.01%). In contrast, costs accelerated for non-durable goods (32.46% vs 31.42%) and energy (26.62% vs 24.93%). On a monthly basis, producer prices rose by 1.52% in July, the lowest in seven months and slowing from a 1.8% gain in the preceding period.

【機構：AI半導體基本面未變 韓股短期或緩慢回升】未來資產證券認為，當前韓國本土股市已接近底部區間。機構指出，近期人工智慧（AI）基礎設施相關個股大幅下跌，並非企業基本面遭到破壞，而是由持倉頭寸集中平倉所導致。 不過，對於股價恢復速度，未來資產證券參考過去市場大跌後的恢復階段，認為此次走勢可能更接近2022年美聯儲加息週期期間的市場修復過程。 該機構表示，在市場持續警惕利率上漲的環境下，很難期待估值水平快速提升，因此相比快速反彈的V型走勢，更可能出現漸進式恢復。券商同時判斷，對韓國本土股市影響力極大的外資後續追加拋售壓力將較為有限。年初至今外資在韓國綜合股價指數累計淨賣出金額達160萬億韓元，創下歷史最高值。上月外資三個月滾動累計淨賣出額佔日均總市值比重為-1.8%，拋售規模與2020年新冠疫情時期、2008至2009年全球金融危機時期處於同一水平。據此未來資產證券預測，本月韓國股市將進入溫和修復階段，核心邏輯為市場估值吸引力提升，且企業盈利並未出現實質性受損。

### 2026-08-03 15:23:13  #MKT News #Market Regions #Greater China

【China's Ministry of Agriculture launches 100-day campaign at national autumn-grain meeting to bolster field management and disaster response】The Ministry of Agriculture held a national autumn-grain production on-site meeting in Dezhou, Shandong, saying autumn grain accounts for the bulk of annual output and the current period is critical for flood/drought control and yield formation. It ordered a 100-day campaign to strengthen field management and disaster resilience, requiring crop- and region-specific fertilizer and water management and 'one-spray, multi-promotion' measures to upgrade seedling conditions. Provinces were told to implement flood-season agricultural disaster-prevention plans, focus on heavy rain/flooding, drought, heat and typhoons, strengthen monitoring and early warning, and use agricultural disaster-relief funds to minimize losses. The ministry also prioritized intensified control of major pests and diseases — rice borers and 'two-migration' migratory pests, corn borers and southern rust — advancing integrated, coordinated and emergency prevention-and-control measures.

Turkey Inflation Rate Hits 4-Month Low — The annual inflation rate eased to 31.75% in July 2026 from 32.11% in the previous month, slightly below market expectations of 31.8%. It marked the lowest reading since March, as prices slowed for housing and utilities (40.32% vs 45.14% in June), transport (30.83% vs 31.15%), alcoholic beverages and tobacco (29.38% vs 34.15%), information and communication (23.97% vs 25.57%), recreation, sport and culture (23.36% vs 25.39%), education (44.18% vs 46.10%), insurance and financial services (25.64% vs 28.07%), and miscellaneous goods and services (21.74% vs 22.71%). Meanwhile, inflation accelerated for food and non-alcoholic beverages (37.53% vs 35.45%), clothing and footwear (16.48% vs 14.18%), furniture and household goods (22.41% vs 22.32%), health (43.89% vs 33.62%), and restaurants and hotels (31.65% vs 31.61%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose to a three-month high of 1.78%, from 0.99% in June, though slightly below market forecasts of 1.83%.

### 2026-08-03 15:20:27  #Trading Economics #Economy #Georgia #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Georgia Inflation Eases to 4-Month Low — Georgia's annual inflation rate eased to 5.5% in July 2025, the lowest level in four months, from 5.8% in June. The main downward pressure came from softer price increases for food and non-alcoholic beverages (4.9% vs 5.6% in June) and transport (14.2% vs 16.5%). Price growth also slowed for restaurants and hotels (7.9% vs 8.4%) and clothing and footwear (1.1% vs 2.1%), while remaining unchanged for health (4.7%) and education (2.4%). Meanwhile, prices continued to decline for furnishings, household equipment and routine household maintenance (-2.4% vs -2.2%) and communication (-6.3% vs -6.4%). In contrast, inflation rose for housing and utilities (8.3% vs 7.2%), recreation and culture (1.0% vs 0.9%), miscellaneous goods and services (7.6% vs 7.3%), and alcoholic beverages and tobacco (5.6% vs 5.4%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices decreased by 0.4% in July, after rising 0.1% in June and marking the first monthly fall since last December.

【中金：AI之外買什麼？從“只買勝率”轉向“兼顧賠率”】中金公司最新研報指出，2026上半年全球市場在AI主線上的極致分化帶來了擁擠與透支。雖然AI產業趨勢未變，長期勝率依然很高，但短期面臨去槓桿和宏觀擾動，市場風格正從“只買勝率”向“兼顧賠率”轉變。研報認為，當前去槓桿已進入中後段，若9月美聯儲加息落地可視作利空出盡，但產業層面仍需等待新場景催化。在此背景下，資金可能流向賠率高且基本面有一定確認度的方向。基於“勝率-賠率”模型，研報給出了以下配置建議：1. 行業配置（MSCI中國）：建議關注綜合打分靠前的五個行業：保險、原材料、電氣裝置、創新藥、電信。2. 跨資產配置：模型顯示，截至7月底打分靠前的資產包括：標普500、納斯達克100、道瓊斯、M7、長端美債、創業板50。

Core inflation excluding energy held at 0.3%. Higher diesel and heating-oil costs were offset by price declines in clothing, car rentals and other goods and services. Insiders said SNB staff expect policy rates to remain at zero through the end of next year absent new shocks.

### 2026-08-03 15:13:27  #Trading Economics #Economy #Austria #Unemployment Rate #Importance 1

Austria Jobless Rate Holds at 6.9% in July — Austria’s unemployment rate remained unchanged at 6.9% in July 2026 from the previous month, while edging up from 6.7% in the corresponding month a year earlier. The total number of unemployed persons rose by 8,917 year-on-year to 298,885. Among them, the female unemployment rate went up by 0.3 percentage points to 6.9%, with the number of unemployed women increasing by 8,038 to 142,244, while the rate for men and other genders remained broadly steady at 6.8%, with the number of unemployed individuals increasing by 879 to 156,641. Dependent employment increased by 9,000 from a year earlier to 4,044,000. Employment among women rose by 6,000 to 1,901,000, whereas employment among men and other genders increase by 3,000 to 2,143,000. Including training participants, the total number of unemployed persons and those enrolled in labour market programs reached 364,202, up by 4,828 from the previous year.

【福建漳州啟動重點食品質量提升行動】近日，福建省漳州市市場監管局召開重點食品質量提升專項行動啟動暨培訓會，推出“診斷+檢測”幫扶模式，全面啟動全市糕點、熟肉製品、餅乾等重點食品品類質量提升幫扶工作。專項行動採取“現場評審+取樣檢測”聯動機制。透過現場核查精準鎖定產品烘焙至內包環節微生物等關鍵風險點，針對生產原料、半成品、裝置器具、從業人員手部等關鍵點位開展靶向取樣檢測，精準溯源指標異常成因。同時建立“一企一檔”動態臺賬，結合排查檢測結果開展綜合研判，推行“一企一策”定製化幫扶，為企業出具專屬整改提升方案，指導企業補齊管理短板、最佳化生產流程、完善防控制度。啟動儀式後，福建省質檢院專家開展食品安全風險防控專題培訓，圍繞重點品類食品質量管控要點、生產過程管控、檢驗檢測比對等關鍵內容進行系統講解，並現場答疑解惑、互動交流。參會企業紛紛表示，培訓內容貼合生產實際、針對性強，對企業自查提質、規範生產具有指導價值，有效補齊了企業技術短板，提升了自主風控能力。（市場監管總局）

【沙特延布港油輪活動激增，紅海石油通道恢復活躍】在胡塞武裝威脅導致地區航運受擾後，沙特關鍵出口港——紅海沿岸的延布港（Yanbu）似乎迎來了最繁忙的一天。與此同時，越來越多船隻關閉追蹤訊號，透過曼德海峽這一重要航運咽喉。根據衛星影象顯示，週六有5艘超大型原油運輸船（VLCC）停靠在延布港石油裝載碼頭，這可能是自兩週前、受伊朗支援的胡塞武裝對沙特港口實施封鎖以來，該港最活躍的一天。這些照片由歐盟“哨兵2號”衛星拍攝。由於該衛星每隔幾天才會經過該區域，因此無法提供連續監測。在伊朗戰爭導致霍爾木茲海峽航運受到嚴重影響後，延布港成為沙特維持大規模原油出口的重要節點。沙特透過管道系統，將每天數百萬桶原油輸送至紅海，再出口至全球市場，從而繞開霍爾木茲海峽這一關鍵航道。

### 2026-08-03 15:08:30  #Trading Economics #Economy #Poland #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

Poland Manufacturing Downturn Eases in July — Poland's S&P Global Manufacturing PMI rose to 49.0 in July 2026 from 46.1 in June, signaling a softer deterioration in factory activity. Output and new orders continued to decline, although at much slower rates, while export orders fell to the weakest pace since January. Employment increased for the first time since April 2025 as firms expanded capacity in anticipation of stronger production, though hiring remained modest. Purchasing activity declined for a third straight month as manufacturers continued to reduce previously accumulated input stocks, while supplier delivery delays eased to their least severe since before the Middle East conflict. Meanwhile, input cost inflation slowed to its weakest pace since February, with output price inflation easing to a four-month low. Business confidence improved from June's 42-month low, supported by expectations of stronger orders, new projects, competitive pricing, and government and EU support programs.

### 2026-08-03 15:07:13  #Trading Economics #Economy #Hungary #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

Hungary Manufacturing Growth Slows in July — Hungary's Manufacturing PMI edged down to 51.4 in July 2026 from an upwardly revised 51.6 in the previous month, remaining above the 50-point threshold that separates expansion from contraction. The reading stayed below its long-term monthly average of 52.6 but exceeded the average July level of 48.9 recorded over the past three years, indicating continued growth in manufacturing activity despite a slower pace. The moderation was driven by weaker new orders, although they remained in expansion territory, while production volumes increased slightly from June. Meanwhile, the purchase price index eased but continued to signal a strong rise in input costs. The employment index strengthened and remained in expansion, with both export and import indicators also pointing to continued growth.

### 2026-08-03 15:01:15  #Trading Economics #Economy #Sweden #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

Sweden Manufacturing Growth Eases to 7-Month Low — Sweden’s Swedbank Manufacturing PMI fell to 55.8 in July 2026 from a downwardly revised 58 in the previous month, marking the lowest level since December 2025 but remaining above its long term average of 54.3. The slowdown was mainly due to weaker order intake (57.5 vs 58.8 in June), production (56.9 vs 59.4), and employment (55.9 vs 57.3). Moreover, suppliers’ delivery times shortened to 60 from 62.7, while inventories increased to 58.2 from 53.6. Export and domestic orders also declined to 54.5 and 54.6, respectively. On the price front, the index measuring suppliers’ raw material and input prices declined sharply to 67.3 from 80.4 a month ago. Manufacturers also became less optimistic, with the production plan index dropping to 65.7 from 68.3, below the 70 threshold, amid concerns that renewed Middle East tensions could reignite cost pressures despite easing input prices.

【跨越11城、惠及6000餘名從業者！食品行業檢驗檢測能力建設與提升培訓計劃收官】7月31日，市場監管總局（國家認監委）與聯合國工業發展組織（UNIDO）聯合指導實施的“提質護航·絲路共信”食品行業檢驗檢測能力建設與提升培訓計劃在北京收官。該培訓計劃旨在深入踐行共建“一帶一路”倡議，推進食品安全國際協同治理。 該培訓計劃是雙方提升中國食品檢驗檢測機構能力、深化共建“一帶一路”國家貿易信任合作的核心舉措。課程設定緊扣政策前沿與技術實戰，涵蓋世界貿易組織（WTO）框架下《技術性貿易壁壘協定》（TBT）和《實施衛生與植物衛生措施協定》（SPS）協議解讀、國際標準化組織（ISO）合格評定標準培訓、食品檢驗檢測監管政策與合規應對、實驗室資質認定及數字化轉型等核心內容。自2025年6月以來，培訓足跡遍佈北京、重慶、西安、南寧、瀋陽、成都、西雙版納、廣州、青島、海口、昆明11座城市，累計線下培訓食品行業檢驗檢測從業人員近1300人，線上培訓學員超過5000人。

ASX 200 Turns Early Weakness to Close Higher — The ASX 200 advanced 43 points, or 0.5%, to end at 9,019 on Monday, reversing early declines and extending last week’s momentum. Investor sentiment improved after U.S. stock futures rallied, buoyed by President Trump’s decision to hold off on further action against Iran. Locally, July factory activity in Australia was revised higher, supported by easing inflation. In main trading partner China, policymakers pledged continued monetary support in H2 2026, vowing to maintain liquidity and adjust tools as needed. Domestic earnings season also drew focus, with AMP, Nick Scali, James Hardie and REA Group set to report, offering insight into corporate resilience amid the five-month Iran war. 4DMedical surged 9.1% to lead health names, while Treasury Wine Estates rose 5.9% atop consumer staples. 【研究公司：DeepSeek的新AI模型是迄今為止執行成本最低的知名模型】據一家研究機構稱，中國初創公司DeepSeek旗下旗艦AI模型的一個版本，在基準測試中執行成本遠低於全球知名模型，且比Anthropic的Claude Fable 5執行成本低100多倍。據訊息人士透露，DeepSeek正籌備潛在的首次公開募股，該公司於上週五正式釋出了其V4-Flash 模型，這是該公司透過發揮其最擅長的優勢——提供超低成本的AI替代方案——來重振發展勢頭的最新嘗試。據研究公司Artificial Analysis稱，DeepSeek的V4-Flash模型每百萬輸入token收費0.14美元，每百萬輸出token收費0.28美元。token是用於衡量AI使用量的資料單位。總部位於舊金山的Artificial Analysis估計，V4-Flash的平均測試成本為每次3美分，而Moonshot AI的Kimi K3為86美分，OpenAI的GPT-5.6 Sol為1.86美元，Claude Fable 5則為3.15美元。

【國管局：紮實開展中央行政事業單位資產核查治理工作】記者3日從國家機關事務管理局獲悉，國管局近期完成中央行政事業單位房屋、土地、公務用車資產核查治理工作。基本摸清了中央行政事業單位房屋、土地、車輛資產底數，推動解決了一批長期存在的歷史遺留問題，為推進“十五五”時期行政事業單位存量國有資產盤活共享奠定良好基礎。據瞭解，為推動整改行政事業性國有資產入賬不夠及時、底數不夠清晰等問題，破解長期以來資產資料數出多源、賬實不符難題，2023年至2026年6月，國管局從打基礎、利長遠出發，組織2萬餘家中央行政事業單位，按照“部門自查—線上複核—現場核查”路徑，對房屋土地實物賬與資產決算報告差異資料、公務用車多套系統間的差異資料開展全面核查治理，將分散的房、地、車資料轉化為資產卡片，建立起一套相對統一、完整的資產卡片庫。（新華社）

### 2026-08-03 14:44:23  #Trading Economics #Economy #Switzerland #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Swiss Inflation Slows to 4-Month Low — Consumer prices in Switzerland rose by 0.4% year-on-year in July 2026, marking the softest increase since March and easing from a 0.5% gain in the previous month. Prices declined mainly for food and non-alcoholic beverages (-1.3% vs -1.2% in June), clothing and footwear (-0.9% vs -0.1%), and household goods and services (-1.7% vs -1.4%). At the same time, price growth moderated for transport (0.8% vs -1.8%), restaurants and hotels (0.5% vs 0.7%), and other goods and services (0.2% vs 0.6%). On the other hand, costs increased for housing and energy (1.4% vs 1.2%), information and communication (0.5% vs 0.3%), and recreation, sport and culture (0.6% vs 0%), while inflation remained steady for education (at 2.6%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices fell by 0.1%, the first decrease in six months, after a flat reading in June. Meanwhile, annual core inflation, which strips out volatile items such as unprocessed food and energy, remained unchanged at 0.3% in July.

### 2026-08-03 14:44:05  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Iron Ore Falls to 15-Month Low — Iron ore futures slipped toward CNY 700 per ton in early August, falling to their lowest level since May 2025 as the lack of forceful policy support in top consumer China added to concerns over a structural downturn in steel demand. The Politburo recently stopped short of announcing major new stimulus measures, instead emphasizing the implementation of existing fiscal policies to support the slowing economy. Analysts said only targeted measures focused on infrastructure spending and selective industrial support are likely to provide meaningful relief for the market. Chinese steel margins have also continued to weaken, while hot metal output extended its decline as construction activity fell to its lowest level since the start of the pandemic. Meanwhile, private data showed China's manufacturing activity slowed to a four-month low in July as output and new orders expanded at a weaker pace, further weighing on the demand outlook.

UK Natural Gas Slides After Trump Halts Iran Strikes — UK natural gas prices dropped more than 4% to around 138 pence per therm on the first trading day of August, the lowest in over two weeks, after President Donald Trump called off a planned weekend attack on Iran, saying Tehran and other Middle Eastern nations had assured him they were working toward a deal. Trump said that negotiations between the parties are set to begin on Monday. The announcement provided some relief after days of escalating tensions that had driven energy prices sharply higher. UK natural gas prices soared more than 38% in July as the renewed fighting heightened concerns over LNG supplies from the Persian Gulf and Europe's ability to replenish gas inventories ahead of winter. European gas facilities ended last month about 55% full, well below both the five-year average and the level recorded at the same time last year, leaving storage levels behind the pace needed to meet pre-winter targets before the heating season begins in November.

### 2026-08-03 14:24:25  #Trading Economics #Economy #Russia #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

Russia Manufacturing Growth Hits 18-Month High — Russia’s S&P Global Manufacturing PMI rose to 50.7 in July from 50.3 in June, signaling the strongest improvement in factory activity since January 2025. Output expanded at the quickest pace since January 2025, while new orders grew at the fastest rate since May 2025, supported by stronger domestic demand. Meanwhile, new export orders fell at the sharpest pace since October 2022. Employment declined further as firms continued not to replace voluntary leavers, though the pace of job shedding eased to a four-month low. Input cost and selling price inflation accelerated, as fuel shortages, transportation issues, and higher supplier costs pushed input cost inflation to its fastest pace since January 2026. Supplier delivery times lengthened to the greatest extent since February 2025 amid logistics disruptions, while firms increased purchasing activity to replenish stocks. Business confidence fell to its lowest level since May 2020 amid customer uncertainty and intensifying competition.

【上海開展2026年政府採購領域“四類”違法違規行為專項整治工作】《關於開展2026年上海市政府採購領域“四類”違法違規行為專項整治工作的通知》對外發布。根據通知，上海市財政局與市公安局、市市場監管局決定聯合開展2026年政府採購領域“四類”違法違規行為專項整治工作。本次專項整治工作的重點內容為：採購人傾斜照顧本地企業，指向特定供應商或特定產品，以供應商註冊地、所有制形式、組織形式、股權結構、投資者國別、經營年限、經營規模、財務指標等不合理條件對供應商實行差別歧視待遇；代理機構違規收費、逾期退還保證金；供應商提供虛假的檢測報告、認證證書、合同業績、中小企業宣告函等材料謀取中標；供應商投標檔案相互混裝、異常一致，投標保證金從同一賬戶轉出，委託同一單位或個人編制投標檔案、辦理投標事宜等惡意串通行為。同時，結合我市工作實際，對依法公開政府採購資訊、落實政府採購政策、及時簽訂採購合同、合同轉包或違法分包、按時支付合同款項等重點監管事項開展監督檢查。

### 2026-08-03 14:12:39  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #Retail Sales MoM #Importance 2

German Retail Sales Fall More than Expected — Germany’s retail sales declined 1.1% month-on-month in June 2026, swinging from a marginally revised 1.2% increase in the previous month and missing market estimates for a 0.5% drop. It marked the sharpest monthly contraction since May 2025, pointing to weaker consumer spending amid persistent economic uncertainty. Food sales fell 1.4%, while non-food sales dropped 1.0%. In contrast, online and mail-order sales rose 0.4%, highlighting continued resilience in e-commerce demand. On an annual basis, retail sales slipped 0.2%, shifting from an upwardly revised 2.1% increase in May and signaling the second time of yearly decline this year.

Rupiah Strengthens Further on Softer Dollar, BI Policy Support — The Indonesian rupiah traded slightly below IDR 18,000 per U.S. dollar on the first trading day of August, extending its winning streak to four straight sessions as the greenback eased further following Japan’s confirmed yen-buying intervention. Locally, headline inflation eased to 2.88% in July, marking a three-month low and well within Bank Indonesia’s 1-1/2%–3-1/2% target and reinforcing expectations that price pressures remain contained. Meanwhile, Bank Indonesia reiterated its commitment to stabilisation through a broad mix beyond rates, including spot FX intervention and onshore and offshore NDFs to manage liquidity and attract foreign inflows, complementing a total 100 bps of hikes since May. However, strength was capped by external concerns following a persistent trade deficit in June, driven by surging imports. Meantime, political uncertainty lingered as President Prabowo has yet to submit candidates for central bank governor following Perry Warjiyo’s abrupt exit.

New Zealand Stocks Close 0.6% Higher — New Zealand's stocks rose 76 points, or 0.6%, to close at 13,774 on Monday, halting losses from the previous session, mainly buoyed by the technology, communication services, and real estate sectors. The broader index tracked gains in US stock futures following an upbeat session on Wall Street on Friday, supported by a rally in technology stocks. Moderating oil prices also supported sentiment, easing inflation concerns and tempering expectations of an interest rate hike. However, traders remained cautious ahead of the release of New Zealand's unemployment data due later this week. Disappointing China's PMI, however, capped gains, with factory activity growing at the slowest pace in four months. On the data front, New Zealand building permits dropped 3.6% in June after an upwardly revised 4.9% fall in May. Among the top movers were Channel Infrastructure (1.5%), Briscoe Group (1.3%), Fisher & Paykel Healthcare (1.1%), Chorus (1.1%), and Freightways Group (1.1%).

【花旗：附帶權益稅制改革料催化香港寫字樓及住宅需求，太古地產及新地將受惠】花旗發表研報指，港府今年6月刊憲並提交了《2026年稅務(修訂)(關於基金、家族投資控權工具及附帶權益)條例草案》，建議最佳化稅務寬減措施，透過擴大基金定義、撤銷附帶交易的5%門檻，以及為附帶權益提供稅務寬減，以吸引私募基金及家族辦公室落戶香港，推動資金及人才流入。花旗認為，改革將成為吸引資金及人才流入香港的結構性催化劑，支援寫字樓及住宅需求，預期寫字樓需求可增長至少5%，並推動新遷入人才增加約2%，同時現有香港資產管理經理可能將稅務節省用於置業，長遠可提升香港國際地位，支援經濟及資產估值增長，相信括置地、太古地產及新鴻基地產可受惠。

### 2026-08-03 13:15:49  #公司 #市場 #國際

【韓國煉油商S-Oil公佈穩健業績，預計原油供應將持續緊張】韓國煉油商S-Oil公佈了穩健的第二季度業績，主要得益於曠日持久的中東衝突使能源價格居高不下、煉油利潤率擴大。該公司預計，今年剩餘時間石油供應將持續緊張。這家由Saudi Aramco控股的煉油商的展望表明，其可能繼續受益於可觀的裂解價差（即原油和成品油之間的價差）。該業績公佈的背景是，繼6月份霍爾木茲海峽封鎖暫時緩解導致油價下跌後，上個月中東緊張局勢再度升溫，推動原油價格反彈。S-Oil週一預測，受亞洲夏季駕車出行旺季需求、酷熱天氣下製冷需求增加以及一些國家出口限制的支撐，第三季度區域煉油利潤率將保持堅挺。該公司表示，由於上半年供應中斷導致全球原油和成品油庫存大幅消耗，供應緊張的局面可能會持續下去，從而為下半年的堅挺市場狀況提供支撐。在截至6月的三個月裡，S-Oil的淨利潤為5,145.8億韓元（相當於3.566億美元）。該數字低於FactSet編制的6,090.2億韓元的平均預期，但與上年同期的虧損相比實現了強勁反彈。收入大增41%，至11.343萬億韓元。營業利潤為9,650.2億韓元，同樣扭轉了上年的虧損局面。

### 2026-08-03 13:14:42  #Trading Economics #Economy #Netherlands #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

Dutch Factory Growth at 3-Month Low — The Nevi Netherlands Manufacturing PMI eased to 54.4 in July 2026 from 55.5 in the previous month, marking the lowest reading since April, but still signalled a strong improvement in operating conditions. The index remained above 50 for the 14th straight month and was among the highest readings in four years. Output growth was the main driver, reaching its fastest pace since February 2022, supported by sustained new order growth and stronger demand. However, total and export order growth softened. Manufacturers increased input purchasing to support production, while stocks of purchases fell as firms used existing inventories. Supply chain disruptions linked to the Middle East conflict persisted, pushing up delivery times and input costs, though inflation eased to four-month lows. Employment continued to rise, backlogs increased for the third time in four months, and confidence recovered above its historical average as firms expected stronger demand and improved economic conditions.

【專家：日美聯合干預日元匯率 下跌趨勢或難扭轉】中國社會科學院美國問題專家馬偉：就效果而言，短期效果會比較顯著，但如果從長期角度分析，其效果值得懷疑。今年4月，日本單月就曾用700多億美元干預匯率，但匯率的反彈只不過是一個月左右的個時間，到了6月就重新回升至160以上。究其原因，根本在於美日之間的利差仍保持較大的水平。日本目前的政策利率只有1%左右，而美國一直維持在3.5%以上。同時，高市政府還在持續的推動財政擴張，推動減稅。市場上對於日本的財政紀律和加息的空間一直也持懷疑態度。而7月的議息會議，雖然美聯儲並沒有加息，但市場預期在未來半年內，美國很有可能會再次開啟加息程序，美日之間的利差有可能再度擴大至2%以上。從中長期角度分析，日元匯率的下行趨勢很難透過兩國的聯合干預而有根本性的改變。（央視）

### 2026-08-03 13:09:18  #Trading Economics #Economy #India #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

India Manufacturing Expands Least in Nearly 5 Years — India's HSBC Manufacturing PMI eased to 53.5 in July 2026 from 54.2 in June, revised down from the preliminary estimate of 53.9, and marking the weakest growth since August 2021. Output and new orders continued to expand, although total sales grew at one of the slowest rates in over four years amid challenging market conditions and softer client demand. Export orders, however, accelerated, supported by stronger demand from Canada, Egypt, Indonesia, Kenya, Nepal, South Africa, Thailand and the UAE. Softer domestic demand also slowed growth in purchasing activity and employment, while input buying rose at the weakest pace in 31 months. Supply-chain conditions improved markedly, with delivery times shortening at a near-record pace. On prices, input cost inflation eased to a five-month low, while output price inflation remained moderate. Business confidence recovered on expectations of stronger demand and infrastructure spending.

【先進封裝行業迎來擴產潮 7只概念股獲機構高頻調研】隨著全球先進封裝產能持續緊缺，國內封測行業迎來大規模擴產潮，長電科技、華天科技、深科技、通富微電、盛合晶微等龍頭企業紛紛擴大先進封裝產能，重點佈局高階儲存、功率半導體、高效能運算封裝賽道。同時，產業鏈將進一步提升生態協同水平，加速國產先進封裝自主可控程序。從市場前景來看，據市場研究機構Yole預估，2025年全球先進封裝的市場規模為540億美元，預計到2031年將增至1090億美元。據資料寶統計，截至目前，今年以來機構調研5次及以上的先進封裝概念股有7只，包括鼎龍股份、通富微電、氣派科技、唯特偶、精智達、藍箭電子、金海通等。鼎龍股份年內累計獲機構調研20次，調研頻次居首。公司最近1次接受機構調研時表示，公司累計佈局四十餘款高階晶圓光刻膠產品，多款型號持續在國內頭部晶圓廠開展驗證測試；目前已有8款ArF、KrF光刻膠實現穩定批次供貨，並持續拓展新增客戶訂單。半導體封裝PI材料已佈局7款產品，其中2款獲得多家客戶訂單。

### 2026-08-03 13:00:38  #其他

【四川再添一現代化智慧電廠】近日，中國能建西南院牽頭總承包的四川能投德陽（中江）燃氣發電工程2號機組完成168小時滿負荷試執行，標誌著該專案全面建成投產。 該專案位於德陽市中江縣，包含兩套先進的“一拖一”單軸燃氣-蒸汽聯合迴圈機組，採用目前世界先進的9H級高效燃機，聯合迴圈效率達63.58%，同步配備煙氣脫硝裝置。 該專案是以調峰為主的清潔能源發電工程，致力打造“低能耗、低排放、高效率”的現代化智慧電廠。其設計年發電量約35.45億千瓦時，可供約105萬戶家庭用電，相比660兆瓦等級燃煤機組，全年可節約標煤約33萬噸，減少二氧化碳排放169萬噸。

【四川：到2026年底，推動阿里雲、四川資料集團等一批萬卡叢集建成投用】近日，四川省發展改革委釋出《四川省推進算力設施最佳化佈局提質建設和算電融合發展實施方案（2026—2028年）》。圍繞完善算力產業生態，《方案》提出，加快推進萬卡級智算叢集規劃建設，積極引入頭部算力企業。到2026年底，推動阿里雲、四川資料集團等一批萬卡叢集建成投用。加快算力晶片國產化部署，引導高密度綠色化機房建設，推進算存網一體化建設，打造一批“詞元示範園區”。

### 2026-08-03 12:53:24  #宏觀

【四川出臺算力發展新方案 到二〇二八年底建成西部算力高地】近日，四川省發展改革委釋出《四川省推進算力設施最佳化佈局提質建設和算電融合發展實施方案（2026—2028年）》（下稱《方案》），明確未來三年全省算力發展“路線圖”。《方案》提出，到2028年底，健全生態體系、建成西部算力高地，智算規模較“十四五”末翻三番、達到240EFLOPS，佔比超過90%。 根據《方案》，四川將構建“一區一帶兩節點”全省算力一體化佈局。 “一區”即成都平原算力核心區，重點發展以訓練、推理一體化智慧算力為主的高能級智算中心。進一步提升天府資料中心叢集（成都）能級，推動國家算力叢集毗鄰連片區域協同發展，支援雅安大資料產業園、德陽天府數谷、中國（綿陽）科技城人工智慧產業園等提檔升級，積極爭取建立國家叢集拓展區。“一帶”即攀西—川西北算電融合帶，依託金沙江上游、雅礱江、大渡河流域水風光一體化清潔能源基地，大力引進龍頭企業，打造重點服務模型大廠需求的算電融合帶，重點發展綠色算力中心。“兩節點”即川南、川東北區域算力節點，服務區域產業發展和城市治理。（四川日報）

### 2026-08-03 12:52:12  #Trading Economics #Economy #Netherlands #Retail Sales YoY #Importance 1

Dutch Retail Sales Growth Slows to 4-Month Low — Retail sales in the Netherlands increased by 2.9% year-on-year in June 2026, easing from an upwardly revised 3.4% in the previous month, marking the weakest growth in four months. Sales growth in the non-food sector moderated to 3.1% from 4.0% in May, as smaller gains in drugstores (4.5% vs. 7.2%), clothing (1.4% vs. 3.6%), DIY, kitchen, and flooring stores (2.2% vs. 3.2%), and recreational items (1.2% vs. 3.7%) outweighed a sharp increase in consumer electronics and white goods (6.3% vs. 1.0%). Additionally, sales declined in furniture and home furnishing stores (-0.5% vs. 1.5%) and shoe and leather goods stores (-4.6% vs. 2.7%). In contrast, turnover for food products grew by 2.5% from 2.1%, lifted by stronger supermarket sales (2.7% vs. 2.1%), while growth in specialty stores eased to 0.9% from 1.9%.

Indonesia Trade Deficit Below Estimates — Indonesia posted a trade deficit of USD 0.45 billion in June 2026, shifting from a USD 4.11 billion surplus in the same month last year and missing expectations of a USD 0.79 billion deficit. It was the second consecutive monthly trade deficit, as import growth outpaced export growth. Imports surged 34.27% year-on-year, exceeding estimates of 25.3% and accelerating from a 22.16% increase in May. Meanwhile, exports grew 8.84%, rebounding from a 5.73% drop in May, which was the steepest decline in six months, and surpassing forecasts of a 0.25% rise.

### 2026-08-03 12:44:35  #Trading Economics #Markets #India #Stock Market #Importance 1

Sensex Rises to 14-Week High — India’s BSE Sensex rose about 0.7% to 78,661 on Monday, extending gains for a fourth straight session and reaching its highest level since April 21, as lower crude oil prices lifted sentiment in the import-dependent economy. Oil prices fell amid optimism over potential US-Iran diplomatic progress, easing concerns about supply disruptions. Investors now await the RBI's policy meeting, where the central bank is widely expected to keep interest rates unchanged. Markets will also watch HSBC manufacturing PMI, forecast to ease to 53.9 in July from 54.2 in June, alongside key US economic releases for further direction. Foreign investors have also returned to Indian equities, with improving growth and earnings expectations supporting sentiment as AI-driven trades lose momentum elsewhere in Asia. Among individual stocks, notable movers included Urban Company (13.6%), Aditya (5.1%), Redington (4.7%), and Divi’s Lab (4.2%). On the losing side, ZEE (-10.7%), and Muthoot (-9.7%) declined.
